===== app/Models/Task.php =====

   1 | <?php
   2 | 
   3 | namespace App\Models;
   4 | 
   5 | use Illuminate\Database\Eloquent\Model;
   6 | use Illuminate\Database\Eloquent\Relations\BelongsTo;
   7 | use Illuminate\Database\Eloquent\Relations\BelongsToMany;
   8 | use Illuminate\Database\Eloquent\Relations\HasMany;
   9 | use Illuminate\Database\Eloquent\SoftDeletes;
  10 | 
  11 | class Task extends Model
  12 | {
  13 |     use SoftDeletes;
  14 | 
  15 |     protected $fillable = [
  16 |         'task_number', 'title', 'description', 'type', 'status', 'priority', 'impact', 'progress',
  17 |         'category', 'start_date', 'due_date', 'completed_at',
  18 |         'reviewed_at', 'blocked_reason', 'estimated_minutes', 'actual_minutes', 'recurrence_rule',
  19 |         'created_by', 'assigned_by',
  20 |         'dossier_id', 'client_id', 'dossier_document_id',
  21 |         'finance_document_id', 'contract_id', 'authorization_id', 'archive_record_id', 'conversation_id',
  22 |         'metadata',
  23 |     ];
  24 | 
  25 |     protected $casts = [
  26 |         'progress' => 'integer',
  27 |         'start_date' => 'date',
  28 |         'due_date' => 'date',
  29 |         'completed_at' => 'datetime',
  30 |         'reviewed_at' => 'datetime',
  31 |         'estimated_minutes' => 'integer',
  32 |         'actual_minutes' => 'integer',
  33 |         'metadata' => 'json',
  34 |     ];
  35 | 
  36 |     public function creator(): BelongsTo { return $this->belongsTo(User::class, 'created_by'); }
  37 |     public function assigner(): BelongsTo { return $this->belongsTo(User::class, 'assigned_by'); }
  38 |     public function assignees(): BelongsToMany { return $this->belongsToMany(User::class, 'task_assignees'); }
  39 |     public function watchers(): BelongsToMany { return $this->belongsToMany(User::class, 'task_watchers'); }
  40 |     public function checklistItems(): HasMany { return $this->hasMany(TaskChecklistItem::class); }
  41 |     public function comments(): HasMany { return $this->hasMany(TaskComment::class); }
  42 |     public function attachments(): HasMany { return $this->hasMany(TaskAttachment::class); }
  43 |     public function activityLogs(): HasMany { return $this->hasMany(TaskActivityLog::class); }
  44 |     public function dossier(): BelongsTo { return $this->belongsTo(Dossier::class); }
  45 |     public function client(): BelongsTo { return $this->belongsTo(Client::class); }
  46 |     public function document(): BelongsTo { return $this->belongsTo(DossierDocument::class, 'dossier_document_id'); }
  47 |     public function financeDocument(): BelongsTo { return $this->belongsTo(FinanceDocument::class); }
  48 |     public function contract(): BelongsTo { return $this->belongsTo(Contract::class); }
  49 |     public function authorization(): BelongsTo { return $this->belongsTo(Authorization::class); }
  50 |     public function archiveRecord(): BelongsTo { return $this->belongsTo(ArchiveRecord::class); }
  51 |     public function conversation(): BelongsTo { return $this->belongsTo(Conversation::class); }
  52 | }

===== app/Http/Controllers/TaskController.php =====

   1 | <?php
   2 | 
   3 | namespace App\Http\Controllers;
   4 | 
   5 | use App\Http\Requests\Task\StoreTaskRequest;
   6 | use App\Http\Requests\Task\UpdateTaskRequest;
   7 | use App\Http\Resources\TaskResource;
   8 | use App\Models\Task;
   9 | use App\Services\Task\TaskMutationService;
  10 | use App\Services\Task\TaskQueryService;
  11 | use Illuminate\Http\RedirectResponse;
  12 | use Illuminate\Http\Request;
  13 | use Illuminate\Validation\Rule;
  14 | use Inertia\Inertia;
  15 | use Inertia\Response;
  16 | 
  17 | class TaskController extends Controller
  18 | {
  19 |     public function __construct(
  20 |         protected TaskQueryService $tasks,
  21 |         protected TaskMutationService $mutations,
  22 |     ) {}
  23 | 
  24 |     public function index(Request $request): Response
  25 |     {
  26 |         $this->authorize('viewAny', Task::class);
  27 | 
  28 |         return Inertia::render('Tasks/Index', $this->tasks->indexPayload($request));
  29 |     }
  30 | 
  31 |     public function store(StoreTaskRequest $request): RedirectResponse
  32 |     {
  33 |         $this->authorize('create', Task::class);
  34 |         $this->mutations->create($request->validated(), $request->user());
  35 | 
  36 |         return redirect()->route('tasks.index')->with('success', 'Task created.');
  37 |     }
  38 | 
  39 |     public function show(Task $task): Response
  40 |     {
  41 |         $this->authorize('view', $task);
  42 | 
  43 |         $task->load([
  44 |             'creator', 'assigner', 'assignees', 'watchers',
  45 |             'checklistItems.completedBy',
  46 |             'comments.user',
  47 |             'attachments.user',
  48 |             'activityLogs.user',
  49 |             'dossier', 'client', 'document', 'financeDocument', 'contract', 'authorization', 'archiveRecord',
  50 |         ]);
  51 |         $task->loadCount(['comments', 'attachments']);
  52 | 
  53 |         return Inertia::render('Tasks/Show', [
  54 |             'task' => new TaskResource($task),
  55 |         ]);
  56 |     }
  57 | 
  58 |     public function update(UpdateTaskRequest $request, Task $task): RedirectResponse
  59 |     {
  60 |         $this->authorize('update', $task);
  61 |         $this->mutations->update($task, $request->validated(), $request->user());
  62 | 
  63 |         return redirect()->back()->with('success', 'Task updated.');
  64 |     }
  65 | 
  66 |     public function destroy(Task $task): RedirectResponse
  67 |     {
  68 |         $this->authorize('delete', $task);
  69 |         $task->delete();
  70 | 
  71 |         return redirect()->route('tasks.index')->with('success', 'Task deleted.');
  72 |     }
  73 | 
  74 |     public function updateStatus(Request $request, Task $task): RedirectResponse
  75 |     {
  76 |         $this->authorize('updateStatus', $task);
  77 |         $data = $request->validate(['status' => ['required', Rule::in(config('archilbo_operations.task_statuses', []))]]);
  78 |         $this->mutations->updateStatus($task, $data['status'], $request->user());
  79 | 
  80 |         return redirect()->back()->with('success', 'Status updated.');
  81 |     }
  82 | }

===== app/Http/Controllers/TaskCommentController.php =====

   1 | <?php
   2 | 
   3 | namespace App\Http\Controllers;
   4 | 
   5 | use App\Http\Requests\Task\StoreCommentRequest;
   6 | use App\Http\Resources\TaskCommentResource;
   7 | use App\Models\Task;
   8 | use App\Models\TaskComment;
   9 | use App\Models\User;
  10 | use App\Services\Task\TaskActivityService;
  11 | use App\Services\Task\TaskNotificationService;
  12 | use Illuminate\Http\RedirectResponse;
  13 | use Illuminate\Http\Request;
  14 | 
  15 | class TaskCommentController extends Controller
  16 | {
  17 |     public function __construct(
  18 |         protected TaskActivityService $activityService,
  19 |         protected TaskNotificationService $notificationService,
  20 |     ) {}
  21 | 
  22 |     public function index(Task $task)
  23 |     {
  24 |         $this->authorize('view', $task);
  25 |         $comments = $task->comments()->with('user')->latest()->get();
  26 |         return TaskCommentResource::collection($comments);
  27 |     }
  28 | 
  29 |     public function store(StoreCommentRequest $request, Task $task): TaskCommentResource
  30 |     {
  31 |         $this->authorize('update', $task);
  32 |         $comment = TaskComment::create([
  33 |             'task_id' => $task->id,
  34 |             'user_id' => $request->user()->id,
  35 |             'body' => $request->validated('body'),
  36 |             'is_note' => $request->validated('is_note', false),
  37 |         ]);
  38 | 
  39 |         $comment->load('user');
  40 | 
  41 |         $desc = $comment->is_note ? 'Note added' : 'Comment added';
  42 |         $this->activityService->log($task, $request->user()->id, $comment->is_note ? 'note_added' : 'comment_added', $desc);
  43 |         $this->notificationService->notifyWatchers($task, 'comment_added', $desc . ' on ' . $task->title);
  44 | 
  45 |         preg_match_all('/@(\w+)/', $comment->body, $matches);
  46 |         if (! empty($matches[1])) {
  47 |             foreach ($matches[1] as $username) {
  48 |                 $mentioned = User::where('name', 'like', "%{$username}%")->first();
  49 |                 if ($mentioned && $mentioned->id !== $request->user()->id) {
  50 |                     $this->notificationService->notifyMentioned($request->user(), $task, $mentioned, 'a comment on ' . $task->title);
  51 |                 }
  52 |             }
  53 |         }
  54 | 
  55 |         return new TaskCommentResource($comment);
  56 |     }
  57 | 
  58 |     public function destroy(Task $task, TaskComment $comment): RedirectResponse
  59 |     {
  60 |         $this->authorize('update', $task);
  61 |         abort_unless($comment->task_id === $task->id, 404);
  62 |         $comment->delete();
  63 |         return redirect()->back()->with('success', 'Comment deleted.');
  64 |     }
  65 | }

===== app/Http/Controllers/TaskChecklistController.php =====

   1 | <?php
   2 | 
   3 | namespace App\Http\Controllers;
   4 | 
   5 | use App\Http\Requests\Task\StoreChecklistRequest;
   6 | use App\Models\Task;
   7 | use App\Models\TaskChecklistItem;
   8 | use App\Services\Task\TaskActivityService;
   9 | use Illuminate\Http\RedirectResponse;
  10 | use Illuminate\Http\Request;
  11 | 
  12 | class TaskChecklistController extends Controller
  13 | {
  14 |     public function __construct(protected TaskActivityService $activityService) {}
  15 | 
  16 |     public function index(Task $task)
  17 |     {
  18 |         $this->authorize('view', $task);
  19 |         return $task->checklistItems()->orderBy('position')->get();
  20 |     }
  21 | 
  22 |     public function store(StoreChecklistRequest $request, Task $task): RedirectResponse
  23 |     {
  24 |         $this->authorize('update', $task);
  25 |         $item = $task->checklistItems()->create([
  26 |             'label' => $request->validated('label'),
  27 |             'position' => $task->checklistItems()->count(),
  28 |         ]);
  29 | 
  30 |         $this->activityService->log($task, $request->user()->id, 'checklist_added', 'Checklist item added: ' . $item->label);
  31 | 
  32 |         return redirect()->back()->with('success', 'Checklist item added.');
  33 |     }
  34 | 
  35 |     public function toggle(Task $task, TaskChecklistItem $item): RedirectResponse
  36 |     {
  37 |         $this->authorize('updateStatus', $task);
  38 |         abort_unless($item->task_id === $task->id, 404);
  39 | 
  40 |         $item->update([
  41 |             'is_done' => ! $item->is_done,
  42 |             'completed_by' => $item->is_done ? null : request()->user()->id,
  43 |             'completed_at' => $item->is_done ? null : now(),
  44 |         ]);
  45 | 
  46 |         $progress = $task->checklistItems()->count() > 0
  47 |             ? round(($task->checklistItems()->where('is_done', true)->count() / $task->checklistItems()->count()) * 100)
  48 |             : 0;
  49 |         $task->update(['progress' => $progress]);
  50 | 
  51 |         $this->activityService->log($task, request()->user()->id, 'checklist_toggled', 'Checklist item ' . ($item->is_done ? 'completed' : 'reopened') . ': ' . $item->label);
  52 | 
  53 |         return redirect()->back()->with('success', 'Checklist updated.');
  54 |     }
  55 | 
  56 |     public function destroy(Task $task, TaskChecklistItem $item): RedirectResponse
  57 |     {
  58 |         $this->authorize('update', $task);
  59 |         abort_unless($item->task_id === $task->id, 404);
  60 |         $item->delete();
  61 |         return redirect()->back()->with('success', 'Checklist item removed.');
  62 |     }
  63 | }

===== app/Http/Controllers/TaskAttachmentController.php =====

   1 | <?php
   2 | 
   3 | namespace App\Http\Controllers;
   4 | 
   5 | use App\Models\Task;
   6 | use App\Models\TaskAttachment;
   7 | use App\Services\Task\TaskActivityService;
   8 | use App\Services\Task\TaskNotificationService;
   9 | use Illuminate\Http\RedirectResponse;
  10 | use Illuminate\Http\Request;
  11 | 
  12 | class TaskAttachmentController extends Controller
  13 | {
  14 |     public function __construct(
  15 |         protected TaskActivityService $activityService,
  16 |         protected TaskNotificationService $notificationService,
  17 |     ) {}
  18 | 
  19 |     public function store(Request $request, Task $task): RedirectResponse
  20 |     {
  21 |         $this->authorize('update', $task);
  22 |         $request->validate([
  23 |             'file' => ['required', 'file', 'max:10240'],
  24 |         ]);
  25 | 
  26 |         $file = $request->file('file');
  27 |         $filename = $file->hashName();
  28 |         $file->storeAs('task-attachments/' . $task->id, $filename);
  29 | 
  30 |         $attachment = TaskAttachment::create([
  31 |             'task_id' => $task->id,
  32 |             'user_id' => $request->user()->id,
  33 |             'filename' => $filename,
  34 |             'original_filename' => $file->getClientOriginalName(),
  35 |             'mime_type' => $file->getMimeType(),
  36 |             'size' => $file->getSize(),
  37 |         ]);
  38 | 
  39 |         $this->activityService->log($task, $request->user()->id, 'attachment_added', 'File attached: ' . $attachment->original_filename);
  40 |         $this->notificationService->notifyWatchers($task, 'file_attached', 'File attached to ' . $task->title);
  41 | 
  42 |         return redirect()->back()->with('success', 'File attached.');
  43 |     }
  44 | 
  45 |     public function destroy(Task $task, TaskAttachment $attachment): RedirectResponse
  46 |     {
  47 |         $this->authorize('update', $task);
  48 |         abort_unless($attachment->task_id === $task->id, 404);
  49 |         $attachment->delete();
  50 |         return redirect()->back()->with('success', 'Attachment removed.');
  51 |     }
  52 | }

  20 |             'impact' => $this->impact,
  21 |             'progress' => $this->progress,

  24 |             'dueDate' => optional($this->due_date)->format('Y-m-d'),
  25 |             'completedAt' => optional($this->completed_at)->toISOString(),

  30 |             'recurrenceRule' => $this->recurrence_rule,

  42 |             'contractId' => $this->contract_id,

  44 |             'archiveRecordId' => $this->archive_record_id,
  45 |             'conversationId' => $this->conversation_id,
  46 |             'dossier' => $this->whenLoaded('dossier', fn () => ['id' => $this->dossier->id, 'number' => $this->dossier->dossier_number, 'object' => $this->dossier->project_object]),
  47 |             'client' => $this->whenLoaded('client', fn () => ['id' => $this->client->id, 'name' => $this->client->full_name]),
  48 |             'createdAt' => $this->created_at?->toISOString(),

===== app/Policies/TaskPolicy.php =====

   1 | <?php
   2 | 
   3 | namespace App\Policies;
   4 | 
   5 | use App\Models\Task;
   6 | use App\Models\User;
   7 | class TaskPolicy
   8 | {
   9 |     public function viewAny(User $user): bool
  10 |     {
  11 |         return $user->can('view tasks') || $user->can('manage tasks') || $user->hasRole('admin');
  12 |     }
  13 | 
  14 |     public function view(User $user, Task $task): bool
  15 |     {
  16 |         if ($user->can('manage tasks') || $user->hasRole('admin')) return true;
  17 |         return $task->assignees()->where('user_id', $user->id)->exists()
  18 |             || $task->watchers()->where('user_id', $user->id)->exists()
  19 |             || $task->created_by === $user->id;
  20 |     }
  21 | 
  22 |     public function create(User $user): bool
  23 |     {
  24 |         return $user->can('manage tasks') || $user->can('view tasks') || $user->hasRole('admin');
  25 |     }
  26 | 
  27 |     public function update(User $user, Task $task): bool
  28 |     {
  29 |         if ($user->can('manage tasks') || $user->hasRole('admin')) return true;
  30 |         return $task->assignees()->where('user_id', $user->id)->exists()
  31 |             || $task->created_by === $user->id;
  32 |     }
  33 | 
  34 |     public function updateStatus(User $user, Task $task): bool
  35 |     {
  36 |         if ($user->can('manage tasks') || $user->hasRole('admin')) return true;
  37 | 
  38 |         return $task->assignees()->where('user_id', $user->id)->exists()
  39 |             || $task->created_by === $user->id;
  40 |     }
  41 | 
  42 |     public function delete(User $user, Task $task): bool
  43 |     {
  44 |         if ($user->can('manage tasks') || $user->hasRole('admin')) return true;
  45 |         return $task->created_by === $user->id;
  46 |     }
  47 | }

===== app\Services\Task\OperationsReportService.php =====

   1 | <?php
   2 | 
   3 | namespace App\Services\Task;
   4 | 
   5 | use App\Models\Task;
   6 | 
   7 | class OperationsReportService
   8 | {
   9 |     public function summary(): array
  10 |     {
  11 |         $base = Task::query();
  12 | 
  13 |         return [
  14 |             'totalTasks' => (clone $base)->count(),
  15 |             'openTasks' => (clone $base)->whereNotIn('status', ['completed', 'cancelled'])->count(),
  16 |             'completedTasks' => (clone $base)->where('status', 'completed')->count(),
  17 |             'blockedTasks' => (clone $base)->where('status', 'blocked')->count(),
  18 |             'overdueTasks' => (clone $base)
  19 |                 ->whereNotNull('due_date')
  20 |                 ->where('due_date', '<', now())
  21 |                 ->whereNotIn('status', ['completed', 'cancelled'])
  22 |                 ->count(),
  23 |             'byModule' => (clone $base)
  24 |                 ->selectRaw('category, count(*) as total')
  25 |                 ->groupBy('category')
  26 |                 ->pluck('total', 'category')
  27 |                 ->all(),
  28 |             'byPriority' => (clone $base)
  29 |                 ->selectRaw('priority, count(*) as total')
  30 |                 ->groupBy('priority')
  31 |                 ->pluck('total', 'priority')
  32 |                 ->all(),
  33 |         ];
  34 |     }
  35 | }

===== app\Services\Task\TaskMutationService.php =====

   1 | <?php
   2 | 
   3 | namespace App\Services\Task;
   4 | 
   5 | use App\Models\Task;
   6 | use App\Models\User;
   7 | 
   8 | class TaskMutationService
   9 | {
  10 |     public function __construct(
  11 |         protected TaskNumberService $numberService,
  12 |         protected TaskActivityService $activityService,
  13 |         protected TaskNotificationService $notificationService,
  14 |     ) {}
  15 | 
  16 |     public function create(array $data, User $user): Task
  17 |     {
  18 |         $assigneeIds = $data['assignee_ids'] ?? [];
  19 |         $watcherIds = $data['watcher_ids'] ?? [];
  20 |         unset($data['assignee_ids'], $data['watcher_ids']);
  21 | 
  22 |         $task = Task::create([
  23 |             ...$data,
  24 |             'task_number' => $data['task_number'] ?? $this->numberService->generate(),
  25 |             'type' => $data['type'] ?? 'general',
  26 |             'impact' => $data['impact'] ?? 'normal',
  27 |             'created_by' => $data['created_by'] ?? $user->id,
  28 |             'assigned_by' => $data['assigned_by'] ?? $user->id,
  29 |         ]);
  30 | 
  31 |         $task->assignees()->sync($assigneeIds);
  32 |         $task->watchers()->sync($watcherIds);
  33 |         $task->load('assignees', 'watchers');
  34 | 
  35 |         $this->activityService->log($task, $user->id, 'created', 'Task created');
  36 |         $task->assignees->each(fn (User $assignee) => $this->notificationService->notifyAssigned($task, $assignee));
  37 | 
  38 |         return $task;
  39 |     }
  40 | 
  41 |     public function update(Task $task, array $data, User $user): Task
  42 |     {
  43 |         $old = $task->replicate();
  44 |         $oldAssigneeIds = $task->assignees()->pluck('users.id')->all();
  45 |         $assigneeIds = $data['assignee_ids'] ?? null;
  46 |         $watcherIds = $data['watcher_ids'] ?? null;
  47 |         unset($data['assignee_ids'], $data['watcher_ids']);
  48 | 
  49 |         $task->update($data);
  50 | 
  51 |         if (is_array($assigneeIds)) {
  52 |             $task->assignees()->sync($assigneeIds);
  53 |             $this->notifyAssigneeChanges($task, $oldAssigneeIds, $assigneeIds);
  54 |         }
  55 | 
  56 |         if (is_array($watcherIds)) {
  57 |             $task->watchers()->sync($watcherIds);
  58 |         }
  59 | 
  60 |         if ($task->status === 'completed' && ! $task->completed_at) {
  61 |             $task->update(['completed_at' => now(), 'progress' => 100]);
  62 |         }
  63 | 
  64 |         $changes = collect($data)
  65 |             ->filter(fn ($value, $key) => $old->$key != $value)
  66 |             ->keys()
  67 |             ->map(fn ($key) => $key . ' changed')
  68 |             ->all();
  69 | 
  70 |         if ($changes) {
  71 |             $this->activityService->log($task, $user->id, 'updated', implode(', ', $changes));
  72 | 
  73 |             if ($old->status !== $task->status) {
  74 |                 $this->notificationService->notifyStatusChanged($task, (string) $old->status, (string) $task->status);
  75 |             }
  76 |         }
  77 | 
  78 |         return $task->refresh();
  79 |     }
  80 | 
  81 |     public function updateStatus(Task $task, string $status, User $user): Task
  82 |     {
  83 |         $oldStatus = (string) $task->status;
  84 | 
  85 |         $task->update([
  86 |             'status' => $status,
  87 |             'completed_at' => $status === 'completed' ? now() : $task->completed_at,
  88 |             'progress' => $status === 'completed' ? 100 : $task->progress,
  89 |             'reviewed_at' => $status === 'in_review' ? null : $task->reviewed_at,
  90 |         ]);
  91 | 
  92 |         $this->activityService->log(
  93 |             $task,
  94 |             $user->id,
  95 |             'status_changed',
  96 |             "Status changed from {$oldStatus} to {$status}",
  97 |             ['status' => $oldStatus],
  98 |             ['status' => $status],
  99 |         );
 100 | 
 101 |         $this->notificationService->notifyStatusChanged($task, $oldStatus, $status);
 102 | 
 103 |         return $task->refresh();
 104 |     }
 105 | 
 106 |     private function notifyAssigneeChanges(Task $task, array $oldIds, array $newIds): void
 107 |     {
 108 |         foreach (array_diff($newIds, $oldIds) as $userId) {
 109 |             if ($user = User::find($userId)) {
 110 |                 $this->notificationService->notifyAssigned($task, $user);
 111 |             }
 112 |         }
 113 | 
 114 |         foreach (array_diff($oldIds, $newIds) as $userId) {
 115 |             if ($user = User::find($userId)) {
 116 |                 $this->notificationService->notifyUser($user, $task, 'unassigned', 'You were unassigned from: ' . $task->title);
 117 |             }
 118 |         }
 119 |     }
 120 | }

===== app\Services\Task\TaskNotificationService.php =====

   1 | <?php
   2 | 
   3 | namespace App\Services\Task;
   4 | 
   5 | use App\Models\Task;
   6 | use App\Models\User;
   7 | use App\Notifications\TaskNotification;
   8 | 
   9 | class TaskNotificationService
  10 | {
  11 |     public function notifyAssignees(Task $task, string $action, string $description): void
  12 |     {
  13 |         $task->assignees->each(fn (User $user) => $user->notify(new TaskNotification($task, $action, $description)));
  14 |     }
  15 | 
  16 |     public function notifyWatchers(Task $task, string $action, string $description): void
  17 |     {
  18 |         $task->watchers->each(fn (User $user) => $user->notify(new TaskNotification($task, $action, $description)));
  19 |     }
  20 | 
  21 |     public function notifyUser(User $user, Task $task, string $action, string $description): void
  22 |     {
  23 |         $user->notify(new TaskNotification($task, $action, $description));
  24 |     }
  25 | 
  26 |     public function notifyAssigned(Task $task, User $assignee): void
  27 |     {
  28 |         $assignee->notify(new TaskNotification($task, 'assigned', 'You have been assigned: ' . $task->title));
  29 |     }
  30 | 
  31 |     public function notifyReassigned(Task $task, User $oldAssignee, User $newAssignee): void
  32 |     {
  33 |         $oldAssignee->notify(new TaskNotification($task, 'reassigned', 'Task ' . $task->task_number . ' was reassigned from you'));
  34 |         $newAssignee->notify(new TaskNotification($task, 'assigned', 'You have been assigned: ' . $task->title));
  35 |     }
  36 | 
  37 |     public function notifyStatusChanged(Task $task, string $oldStatus, string $newStatus): void
  38 |     {
  39 |         $action = match ($newStatus) {
  40 |             'completed' => 'completed',
  41 |             'in_review' => 'in_review',
  42 |             'blocked' => 'blocked',
  43 |             default => 'status_changed',
  44 |         };
  45 |         $desc = "Task {$task->task_number} moved from {$oldStatus} to {$newStatus}";
  46 |         $this->notifyAssignees($task, $action, $desc);
  47 |         $this->notifyWatchers($task, $action, $desc);
  48 |     }
  49 | 
  50 |     public function notifyOverdue(Task $task): void
  51 |     {
  52 |         $desc = 'Task ' . $task->task_number . ' is overdue (due: ' . ($task->due_date?->format('Y-m-d') ?? 'N/A') . ')';
  53 |         $this->notifyAssignees($task, 'overdue', $desc);
  54 |         $this->notifyWatchers($task, 'overdue', $desc);
  55 |     }
  56 | 
  57 |     public function notifyDueTomorrow(Task $task): void
  58 |     {
  59 |         $desc = 'Task ' . $task->task_number . ' is due tomorrow';
  60 |         $this->notifyAssignees($task, 'due_tomorrow', $desc);
  61 |     }
  62 | 
  63 |     public function notifyMentioned(User $mentionedBy, Task $task, User $mentionedUser, string $context): void
  64 |     {
  65 |         $mentionedUser->notify(new TaskNotification($task, 'mentioned', $mentionedBy->name . ' mentioned you in ' . $context));
  66 |     }
  67 | 
  68 |     public function notifySuggestionCreated(string $description, ?User $user = null): void
  69 |     {
  70 |         if ($user) {
  71 |             $user->notify(new \App\Notifications\SuggestionNotification($description));
  72 |         }
  73 |     }
  74 | }

===== app\Services\Task\TaskNumberService.php =====

   1 | <?php
   2 | 
   3 | namespace App\Services\Task;
   4 | 
   5 | use App\Models\Task;
   6 | 
   7 | class TaskNumberService
   8 | {
   9 |     public function generate(): string
  10 |     {
  11 |         $prefix = 'TASK-';
  12 |         $last = Task::withTrashed()->where('task_number', 'like', $prefix . date('Y') . '-%')
  13 |             ->orderBy('id', 'desc')->value('task_number');
  14 | 
  15 |         if ($last) {
  16 |             $num = (int) substr($last, strrpos($last, '-') + 1) + 1;
  17 |         } else {
  18 |             $num = 1;
  19 |         }
  20 | 
  21 |         return $prefix . date('Y') . '-' . str_pad((string) $num, 4, '0', STR_PAD_LEFT);
  22 |     }
  23 | }

===== app\Services\Task\TaskPermissionService.php =====

   1 | <?php
   2 | 
   3 | namespace App\Services\Task;
   4 | 
   5 | use App\Models\Task;
   6 | use App\Models\User;
   7 | 
   8 | class TaskPermissionService
   9 | {
  10 |     public function canView(?User $user, Task $task): bool
  11 |     {
  12 |         if (! $user) return false;
  13 |         if ($user->can('manage tasks') || $user->hasRole('admin')) return true;
  14 |         return $task->assignees()->where('user_id', $user->id)->exists()
  15 |             || $task->watchers()->where('user_id', $user->id)->exists()
  16 |             || $task->created_by === $user->id;
  17 |     }
  18 | 
  19 |     public function canUpdate(?User $user, Task $task): bool
  20 |     {
  21 |         if (! $user) return false;
  22 |         if ($user->can('manage tasks') || $user->hasRole('admin')) return true;
  23 |         return $task->assignees()->where('user_id', $user->id)->exists()
  24 |             || $task->created_by === $user->id;
  25 |     }
  26 | 
  27 |     public function canDelete(?User $user, Task $task): bool
  28 |     {
  29 |         if (! $user) return false;
  30 |         return $user->can('manage tasks') || $user->hasRole('admin') || $task->created_by === $user->id;
  31 |     }
  32 | }

===== app\Services\Task\TaskQueryService.php =====

   1 | <?php
   2 | 
   3 | namespace App\Services\Task;
   4 | 
   5 | use App\Http\Resources\TaskResource;
   6 | use App\Models\Task;
   7 | use App\Models\User;
   8 | use Illuminate\Http\Request;
   9 | 
  10 | class TaskQueryService
  11 | {
  12 |     public function __construct(protected TaskRequestService $taskRequests) {}
  13 | 
  14 |     public function indexPayload(Request $request): array
  15 |     {
  16 |         $user = $request->user();
  17 |         $filter = $request->query('filter', 'all');
  18 |         $category = $request->query('category', 'all');
  19 |         $search = $request->query('search');
  20 | 
  21 |         $query = Task::query()
  22 |             ->with(['assignees', 'watchers', 'creator', 'assigner', 'checklistItems', 'dossier', 'client'])
  23 |             ->withCount(['comments', 'attachments']);
  24 | 
  25 |         if (! $user->can('manage tasks') && ! $user->hasRole('admin')) {
  26 |             $query->where(function ($q) use ($user) {
  27 |                 $q->where('created_by', $user->id)
  28 |                     ->orWhereHas('assignees', fn ($assignees) => $assignees->where('user_id', $user->id))
  29 |                     ->orWhereHas('watchers', fn ($watchers) => $watchers->where('user_id', $user->id));
  30 |             });
  31 |         }
  32 | 
  33 |         match ($filter) {
  34 |             'my' => $query->whereHas('assignees', fn ($q) => $q->where('user_id', $user->id)),
  35 |             'assigned_by_me' => $query->where('assigned_by', $user->id),
  36 |             'watching' => $query->whereHas('watchers', fn ($q) => $q->where('user_id', $user->id)),
  37 |             'overdue' => $query->whereNotNull('due_date')->where('due_date', '<', now())->whereNotIn('status', ['completed', 'cancelled']),
  38 |             'due_today' => $query->whereDate('due_date', today()),
  39 |             'due_week' => $query->whereBetween('due_date', [now()->startOfWeek(), now()->endOfWeek()]),
  40 |             'blocked' => $query->where('status', 'blocked'),
  41 |             'completed' => $query->where('status', 'completed'),
  42 |             default => null,
  43 |         };
  44 | 
  45 |         if ($category !== 'all') {
  46 |             $query->where('category', $category);
  47 |         }
  48 | 
  49 |         if ($search) {
  50 |             $query->where(function ($q) use ($search) {
  51 |                 $q->where('title', 'like', "%{$search}%")
  52 |                     ->orWhere('task_number', 'like', "%{$search}%")
  53 |                     ->orWhere('description', 'like', "%{$search}%");
  54 |             });
  55 |         }
  56 | 
  57 |         $tasks = $query->latest()->get();
  58 | 
  59 |         return [
  60 |             'tasks' => TaskResource::collection($tasks)->resolve(),
  61 |             'users' => User::query()
  62 |                 ->orderBy('name')
  63 |                 ->get(['id', 'name', 'email'])
  64 |                 ->map(fn (User $user) => [
  65 |                     'id' => $user->id,
  66 |                     'name' => $user->name,
  67 |                     'email' => $user->email,
  68 |                 ]),
  69 |             'activeFilter' => $filter,
  70 |             'activeCategory' => $category,
  71 |             'operationsConfig' => [
  72 |                 'statuses' => config('archilbo_operations.task_statuses', []),
  73 |                 'types' => config('archilbo_operations.task_types', []),
  74 |                 'categories' => config('archilbo_operations.task_categories', []),
  75 |                 'priorities' => config('archilbo_operations.task_priorities', []),
  76 |                 'impacts' => config('archilbo_operations.task_impacts', []),
  77 |             ],
  78 |             'taskRequestTypes' => config('archilbo_operations.task_request_types', []),
  79 |             'taskRequestTypeLabels' => config('archilbo_operations.task_request_type_labels', []),
  80 |             'taskRequestOptions' => $this->taskRequests->formOptions(),
  81 |         ];
  82 |     }
  83 | }

===== app\Services\Task\TaskRequestService.php =====

   1 | <?php
   2 | 
   3 | namespace App\Services\Task;
   4 | 
   5 | use App\Models\Client;
   6 | use App\Models\Dossier;
   7 | use App\Models\TaskRequest;
   8 | use App\Models\User;
   9 | use Illuminate\Validation\ValidationException;
  10 | 
  11 | class TaskRequestService
  12 | {
  13 |     public function __construct(protected TaskMutationService $tasks) {}
  14 | 
  15 |     public function listFor(User $user): array
  16 |     {
  17 |         $query = TaskRequest::query()
  18 |             ->with(['requester', 'targetUser', 'client', 'dossier'])
  19 |             ->latest();
  20 | 
  21 |         if (! $user->can('manage task requests') && ! $user->hasRole('admin')) {
  22 |             $query->where(function ($q) use ($user) {
  23 |                 $q->where('requested_by', $user->id)
  24 |                     ->orWhere('target_user_id', $user->id);
  25 |             });
  26 |         }
  27 | 
  28 |         return $query->get()->all();
  29 |     }
  30 | 
  31 |     public function formOptions(): array
  32 |     {
  33 |         return [
  34 |             'users' => User::query()
  35 |                 ->orderBy('name')
  36 |                 ->get(['id', 'name', 'email'])
  37 |                 ->map(fn (User $user) => [
  38 |                     'id' => $user->id,
  39 |                     'label' => $user->name,
  40 |                     'description' => $user->email,
  41 |                 ])
  42 |                 ->values(),
  43 |             'clients' => Client::query()
  44 |                 ->orderBy('full_name')
  45 |                 ->limit(150)
  46 |                 ->get(['id', 'full_name', 'client_number'])
  47 |                 ->map(fn (Client $client) => [
  48 |                     'id' => $client->id,
  49 |                     'label' => trim(($client->client_number ? $client->client_number . ' - ' : '') . $client->full_name),
  50 |                 ])
  51 |                 ->values(),
  52 |             'dossiers' => Dossier::query()
  53 |                 ->with('client:id,full_name')
  54 |                 ->orderBy('dossier_number')
  55 |                 ->limit(200)
  56 |                 ->get(['id', 'client_id', 'dossier_number', 'project_object'])
  57 |                 ->map(fn (Dossier $dossier) => [
  58 |                     'id' => $dossier->id,
  59 |                     'clientId' => $dossier->client_id,
  60 |                     'label' => trim($dossier->dossier_number . ' - ' . ($dossier->project_object ?? '-') . ' - ' . ($dossier->client?->full_name ?? '-')),
  61 |                 ])
  62 |                 ->values(),
  63 |         ];
  64 |     }
  65 | 
  66 |     public function nextNumber(): string
  67 |     {
  68 |         $prefix = 'REQ-' . now()->format('Y') . '-';
  69 |         $last = TaskRequest::query()
  70 |             ->where('request_number', 'like', $prefix . '%')
  71 |             ->latest('id')
  72 |             ->value('request_number');
  73 | 
  74 |         $next = $last ? ((int) substr($last, -4)) + 1 : 1;
  75 | 
  76 |         return $prefix . str_pad((string) $next, 4, '0', STR_PAD_LEFT);
  77 |     }
  78 | 
  79 |     public function create(array $data, User $requester): TaskRequest
  80 |     {
  81 |         return TaskRequest::create([
  82 |             ...$data,
  83 |             'request_number' => $data['request_number'] ?? $this->nextNumber(),
  84 |             'requested_by' => $data['requested_by'] ?? $requester->id,
  85 |             'status' => $data['status'] ?? 'submitted',
  86 |         ]);
  87 |     }
  88 | 
  89 |     public function accept(TaskRequest $taskRequest): TaskRequest
  90 |     {
  91 |         $this->ensureCanChangeDecision($taskRequest);
  92 | 
  93 |         if ($taskRequest->status === 'accepted') {
  94 |             return $taskRequest->refresh();
  95 |         }
  96 | 
  97 |         $taskRequest->update(['status' => 'accepted']);
  98 | 
  99 |         return $taskRequest->refresh();
 100 |     }
 101 | 
 102 |     public function reject(TaskRequest $taskRequest, ?string $reason = null): TaskRequest
 103 |     {
 104 |         $this->ensureCanChangeDecision($taskRequest);
 105 | 
 106 |         $metadata = $taskRequest->metadata ?? [];
 107 | 
 108 |         if ($reason) {
 109 |             $metadata['rejection_reason'] = $reason;
 110 |         }
 111 | 
 112 |         $taskRequest->update([
 113 |             'status' => 'rejected',
 114 |             'metadata' => $metadata,
 115 |         ]);
 116 | 
 117 |         return $taskRequest->refresh();
 118 |     }
 119 | 
 120 |     public function markConverted(TaskRequest $taskRequest, int $taskId): TaskRequest
 121 |     {
 122 |         $taskRequest->update([
 123 |             'status' => 'converted',
 124 |             'converted_task_id' => $taskId,
 125 |         ]);
 126 | 
 127 |         return $taskRequest->refresh();
 128 |     }
 129 | 
 130 |     public function convertToTask(TaskRequest $taskRequest, User $user): TaskRequest
 131 |     {
 132 |         if ($taskRequest->status === 'rejected') {
 133 |             throw ValidationException::withMessages([
 134 |                 'task_request' => 'Rejected requests cannot be converted.',
 135 |             ]);
 136 |         }
 137 | 
 138 |         if ($taskRequest->converted_task_id) {
 139 |             return $taskRequest->refresh();
 140 |         }
 141 | 
 142 |         $mapping = config("archilbo_operations.task_request_task_mapping.{$taskRequest->request_type}", [
 143 |             'type' => 'internal_admin',
 144 |             'category' => 'general_admin',
 145 |         ]);
 146 | 
 147 |         $task = $this->tasks->create([
 148 |             'title' => $taskRequest->title,
 149 |             'description' => $taskRequest->description,
 150 |             'type' => $mapping['type'],
 151 |             'category' => $mapping['category'],
 152 |             'status' => 'not_started',
 153 |             'priority' => 'medium',
 154 |             'impact' => 'normal',
 155 |             'assignee_ids' => array_values(array_filter([$taskRequest->target_user_id])),
 156 |             'watcher_ids' => array_values(array_filter([$taskRequest->requested_by])),
 157 |             'client_id' => $taskRequest->client_id,
 158 |             'dossier_id' => $taskRequest->dossier_id,
 159 |             'dossier_document_id' => $taskRequest->dossier_document_id,
 160 |             'finance_document_id' => $taskRequest->finance_document_id,
 161 |             'contract_id' => $taskRequest->contract_id,
 162 |             'authorization_id' => $taskRequest->authorization_id,
 163 |             'archive_record_id' => $taskRequest->archive_record_id,
 164 |         ], $user);
 165 | 
 166 |         return $this->markConverted($taskRequest, $task->id);
 167 |     }
 168 | 
 169 |     private function ensureCanChangeDecision(TaskRequest $taskRequest): void
 170 |     {
 171 |         if (in_array($taskRequest->status, ['rejected', 'converted'], true)) {
 172 |             throw ValidationException::withMessages([
 173 |                 'task_request' => 'This request is already closed.',
 174 |             ]);
 175 |         }
 176 |     }
 177 | }

===== app\Services\Task\TaskSuggestionService.php =====

   1 | <?php
   2 | 
   3 | namespace App\Services\Task;
   4 | 
   5 | use App\Models\Client;
   6 | use App\Models\Contract;
   7 | use App\Models\Dossier;
   8 | use App\Models\DossierDocument;
   9 | use App\Models\FinanceDocument;
  10 | use App\Models\TaskSuggestion;
  11 | use Carbon\Carbon;
  12 | 
  13 | class TaskSuggestionService
  14 | {
  15 |     public function generateForDossier(Dossier $dossier): array
  16 |     {
  17 |         $suggestions = [];
  18 | 
  19 |         if ($this->isMissingRequiredDocuments($dossier)) {
  20 |             $suggestions[] = $this->make('missing_document', 'Upload missing required documents for ' . ($dossier->project_object ?? 'dossier'), $dossier);
  21 |         }
  22 | 
  23 |         if ($this->isContractReadyButNotGenerated($dossier)) {
  24 |             $suggestions[] = $this->make('contract_ready', 'Contract is ready but not yet generated for ' . ($dossier->project_object ?? 'dossier'), $dossier);
  25 |         }
  26 | 
  27 |         if ($this->isAuthorizationPendingTooLong($dossier)) {
  28 |             $suggestions[] = $this->make('authorization_pending', 'Authorization has been pending too long â€” follow up', $dossier);
  29 |         }
  30 | 
  31 |         $financeSuggestions = $this->checkFinanceOverdue($dossier);
  32 |         foreach ($financeSuggestions as $s) {
  33 |             $suggestions[] = $s;
  34 |         }
  35 | 
  36 |         if ($this->isProjectStuck($dossier)) {
  37 |             $suggestions[] = $this->make('project_stuck', 'Project stuck in "' . ($dossier->workflow_step ?? 'current') . '" step â€” review blocker', $dossier);
  38 |         }
  39 | 
  40 |         if ($this->isArchiveMissing($dossier)) {
  41 |             $suggestions[] = $this->make('archive_missing', 'Project is closed but archive record is missing', $dossier);
  42 |         }
  43 | 
  44 |         if ($this->isCinExpiringSoon($dossier)) {
  45 |             $suggestions[] = $this->make('cin_expiring', 'Client CIN is expiring soon â€” request renewal', $dossier);
  46 |         }
  47 | 
  48 |         return $suggestions;
  49 |     }
  50 | 
  51 |     public function createSuggestion(string $type, string $description, ?Dossier $dossier = null, mixed $related = null): TaskSuggestion
  52 |     {
  53 |         return TaskSuggestion::create([
  54 |             'type' => $type,
  55 |             'description' => $description,
  56 |             'dossier_id' => $dossier?->id,
  57 |             'client_id' => $dossier?->client_id,
  58 |             'related_entity_id' => $related?->id,
  59 |             'related_entity_type' => $related ? get_class($related) : null,
  60 |         ]);
  61 |     }
  62 | 
  63 |     private function make(string $type, string $desc, Dossier $dossier): TaskSuggestion
  64 |     {
  65 |         return $this->createSuggestion($type, $desc, $dossier);
  66 |     }
  67 | 
  68 |     private function isMissingRequiredDocuments(Dossier $dossier): bool
  69 |     {
  70 |         $required = $dossier->workflowRequirements()->where('step_key', 'documents')->where('is_done', false)->count();
  71 |         return $required > 0;
  72 |     }
  73 | 
  74 |     private function isContractReadyButNotGenerated(Dossier $dossier): bool
  75 |     {
  76 |         if ($dossier->contract()->where('status', 'signed')->exists()) return false;
  77 |         if ($dossier->contract()->where('status', 'generated')->exists()) return false;
  78 |         $hasDraft = $dossier->contract()->where('status', 'draft')->exists();
  79 |         return $hasDraft;
  80 |     }
  81 | 
  82 |     private function isAuthorizationPendingTooLong(Dossier $dossier): bool
  83 |     {
  84 |         $pending = $dossier->authorization()->whereIn('status', ['submitted', 'under_review'])->first();
  85 |         if (! $pending) return false;
  86 |         return $pending->created_at->diffInDays(now()) > 14;
  87 |     }
  88 | 
  89 |     private function checkFinanceOverdue(Dossier $dossier): array
  90 |     {
  91 |         $res = [];
  92 |         $overdue = $dossier->financeDocuments()->where('status', 'overdue')->get();
  93 |         foreach ($overdue as $doc) {
  94 |             $res[] = $this->createSuggestion('finance_overdue', 'Invoice ' . ($doc->number ?? '#') . ' is overdue â€” payment follow-up required', $dossier, $doc);
  95 |         }
  96 |         $due = $dossier->financeDocuments()->where('status', 'sent')->whereNotNull('due_date')->get();
  97 |         foreach ($due as $doc) {
  98 |             if (Carbon::parse($doc->due_date)->diffInDays(now()) <= 3) {
  99 |                 $res[] = $this->createSuggestion('finance_due_soon', 'Invoice ' . ($doc->number ?? '#') . ' is due soon â€” prepare reminder', $dossier, $doc);
 100 |             }
 101 |         }
 102 |         return $res;
 103 |     }
 104 | 
 105 |     private function isProjectStuck(Dossier $dossier): bool
 106 |     {
 107 |         if ($dossier->status === 'closed') return false;
 108 |         return $dossier->updated_at->diffInDays(now()) > 21;
 109 |     }
 110 | 
 111 |     private function isArchiveMissing(Dossier $dossier): bool
 112 |     {
 113 |         if ($dossier->status !== 'closed') return false;
 114 |         return ! $dossier->archiveRecord()->exists();
 115 |     }
 116 | 
 117 |     private function isCinExpiringSoon(Dossier $dossier): bool
 118 |     {
 119 |         $client = $dossier->client;
 120 |         if (! $client || ! $client->cni_expiration_date) return false;
 121 |         return Carbon::parse($client->cni_expiration_date)->diffInDays(now()) <= 30;
 122 |     }
 123 | }

===== app\Services\Task\WorkloadService.php =====

   1 | <?php
   2 | 
   3 | namespace App\Services\Task;
   4 | 
   5 | use App\Models\User;
   6 | 
   7 | class WorkloadService
   8 | {
   9 |     public function summary(): array
  10 |     {
  11 |         return User::query()
  12 |             ->withCount([
  13 |                 'tasksAssigned as open_tasks_count' => fn ($query) => $query->whereNotIn('status', ['completed', 'cancelled']),
  14 |                 'tasksAssigned as urgent_tasks_count' => fn ($query) => $query->where('priority', 'urgent')->whereNotIn('status', ['completed', 'cancelled']),
  15 |                 'tasksAssigned as blocked_tasks_count' => fn ($query) => $query->where('status', 'blocked'),
  16 |                 'tasksAssigned as overdue_tasks_count' => fn ($query) => $query->whereNotNull('due_date')->where('due_date', '<', now())->whereNotIn('status', ['completed', 'cancelled']),
  17 |             ])
  18 |             ->orderBy('name')
  19 |             ->get()
  20 |             ->map(fn (User $user) => [
  21 |                 'userId' => $user->id,
  22 |                 'name' => $user->name,
  23 |                 'email' => $user->email,
  24 |                 'openTasks' => (int) $user->open_tasks_count,
  25 |                 'urgentTasks' => (int) $user->urgent_tasks_count,
  26 |                 'blockedTasks' => (int) $user->blocked_tasks_count,
  27 |                 'overdueTasks' => (int) $user->overdue_tasks_count,
  28 |             ])
  29 |             ->values()
  30 |             ->all();
  31 |     }
  32 | }

===== resources/js/pages/Tasks/Index.tsx =====

   1 | import { Head, router } from '@inertiajs/react';

   3 | import { FormEvent, useCallback, useEffect, useMemo, useState } from 'react';
   4 | import { toast } from 'sonner';
   5 | import { AppShell } from '@/components/layout/AppShell';
   6 | import { AppButton } from '@/components/ui/AppButton';
   7 | import type { FormErrors } from '@/lib/formErrors';
   8 | import type { TaskRow, TaskStatus, UserOption } from '@/features/tasks/types';
   9 | import { COLUMNS } from '@/features/tasks/types';
  10 | import { TaskFilters } from '@/features/tasks/components/TaskFilters';
  11 | import { TaskBoard } from '@/features/tasks/components/TaskBoard';
  12 | import { TaskCalendar } from '@/features/tasks/components/TaskCalendar';
  13 | import { TaskList } from '@/features/tasks/components/TaskList';
  14 | import { TaskCreateDrawer } from '@/features/tasks/components/TaskCreateDrawer';
  15 | import { TaskDetailDrawer } from '@/features/tasks/components/TaskDetailDrawer';
  16 | import { TaskRequestCreateDrawer, type TaskRequestOptions } from '@/features/tasks/components/TaskRequestCreateDrawer';
  17 | 
  18 | type PageProps = {
  19 |     tasks: TaskRow[];
  20 |     users: UserOption[];
  21 |     activeFilter: string;
  22 |     activeCategory: string;
  23 |     taskRequestTypes: string[];
  24 |     taskRequestTypeLabels: Record<string, string>;
  25 |     taskRequestOptions: TaskRequestOptions;
  26 | };
  27 | 
  28 | function isOpenTask(task: TaskRow) {
  29 |     return task.status !== 'completed' && task.status !== 'cancelled';
  30 | }
  31 | 
  32 | function isOverdue(task: TaskRow) {
  33 |     return Boolean(task.dueDate && new Date(task.dueDate) < new Date() && isOpenTask(task));
  34 | }
  35 | 
  36 | function OperationsMetric({
  37 |     label,
  38 |     value,
  39 |     helper,
  40 |     tone,
  41 |     icon: Icon,
  42 | }: {
  43 |     label: string;
  44 |     value: string | number;
  45 |     helper: string;
  46 |     tone: 'gold' | 'red' | 'green' | 'blue';
  47 |     icon: typeof Flame;
  48 | }) {
  49 |     const colors = {
  50 |         gold: 'text-[var(--crm-gold)] bg-[var(--crm-gold-soft)]',
  51 |         red: 'text-red-300 bg-red-500/10',
  52 |         green: 'text-emerald-300 bg-emerald-500/10',
  53 |         blue: 'text-blue-300 bg-blue-500/10',
  54 |     };
  55 | 
  56 |     return (
  57 |         <div className="rounded-xl border border-[var(--crm-border)] bg-[linear-gradient(135deg,var(--crm-elevated),rgba(0,0,0,0.18))] p-3">
  58 |             <div className="flex items-center justify-between gap-3">
  59 |                 <div>
  60 |                     <p className="text-[10px] font-black uppercase tracking-[0.14em] text-[var(--crm-muted)]">{label}</p>
  61 |                     <p className="mt-1 text-2xl font-black leading-none text-[var(--crm-text)]">{value}</p>
  62 |                     <p className="mt-1 truncate text-[11px] text-[var(--crm-muted)]">{helper}</p>
  63 |                 </div>
  64 |                 <span className={`flex size-8 shrink-0 items-center justify-center rounded-lg ${colors[tone]}`}>
  65 |                     <Icon size={16} />
  66 |                 </span>
  67 |             </div>
  68 |         </div>
  69 |     );
  70 | }
  71 | 
  72 | export default function TasksIndex({ tasks, users, activeFilter, activeCategory, taskRequestTypes, taskRequestTypeLabels, taskRequestOptions }: PageProps) {
  73 |     const [localTasks, setLocalTasks] = useState<TaskRow[]>(tasks);
  74 |     const [query, setQuery] = useState('');
  75 |     const [filter, setFilter] = useState(activeFilter);
  76 |     const [category, setCategory] = useState(activeCategory);
  77 |     const [viewMode, setViewMode] = useState<'board' | 'list' | 'calendar'>('board');
  78 |     const [selectedTask, setSelectedTask] = useState<TaskRow | null>(null);
  79 |     const [createOpen, setCreateOpen] = useState(false);
  80 |     const [requestOpen, setRequestOpen] = useState(false);
  81 |     const [form, setForm] = useState({
  82 |         title: '', description: '', status: 'not_started' as string,
  83 |         priority: 'medium' as string, category: 'general_admin' as string,
  84 |         due_date: '', assignee_ids: [] as number[], watcher_ids: [] as number[],
  85 |     });
  86 |     const [formErrors, setFormErrors] = useState<FormErrors>({});
  87 | 
  88 |     const filtered = useMemo(() => {
  89 |         let items = localTasks;
  90 |         if (query.trim()) {
  91 |             const q = query.toLowerCase();
  92 |             items = items.filter((t) => t.title.toLowerCase().includes(q) || t.taskNumber.toLowerCase().includes(q));
  93 |         }
  94 |         if (category !== 'all') {
  95 |             items = items.filter((t) => t.category === category);
  96 |         }
  97 |         return items;
  98 |     }, [localTasks, query, category]);
  99 | 
 100 |     const columns = useMemo(() => {
 101 |         const map: Record<string, TaskRow[]> = {};
 102 |         for (const col of COLUMNS) map[col] = [];
 103 |         for (const t of filtered) {
 104 |             if (map[t.status]) map[t.status].push(t);
 105 |         }
 106 |         return map;
 107 |     }, [filtered]);
 108 | 
 109 |     const metrics = useMemo(() => {
 110 |         const open = localTasks.filter(isOpenTask);
 111 |         const overdue = localTasks.filter(isOverdue);
 112 |         const urgent = open.filter((task) => task.priority === 'urgent' || task.impact === 'critical');
 113 |         const blocked = open.filter((task) => task.status === 'blocked');
 114 |         const review = open.filter((task) => task.status === 'in_review');
 115 |         const completed = localTasks.filter((task) => task.status === 'completed');
 116 | 
 117 |         return { open, overdue, urgent, blocked, review, completed };
 118 |     }, [localTasks]);
 119 | 
 120 |     useEffect(() => {
 121 |         setFilter(activeFilter);
 122 |     }, [activeFilter]);
 123 | 
 124 |     useEffect(() => {
 125 |         setLocalTasks(tasks);
 126 |         setSelectedTask((current) => current ? tasks.find((task) => task.id === current.id) ?? current : null);
 127 |     }, [tasks]);
 128 | 
 129 |     const handleCreate = useCallback((event: FormEvent<HTMLFormElement>) => {
 130 |         event.preventDefault();
 131 |         setFormErrors({});
 132 |         router.post('/tasks', form, {
 133 |             preserveScroll: true,
 134 |             onSuccess: () => {
 135 |                 setCreateOpen(false);
 136 |                 setForm({ title: '', description: '', status: 'not_started', priority: 'medium', category: 'general_admin', due_date: '', assignee_ids: [], watcher_ids: [] });
 137 |                 toast.success('Task created.');
 138 |             },
 139 |             onError: (err) => {
 140 |                 setFormErrors(err);
 141 |                 toast.error('Please check form errors.');
 142 |             },
 143 |         });
 144 |     }, [form]);
 145 | 
 146 |     const replaceTask = useCallback((taskId: number, updater: (task: TaskRow) => TaskRow) => {
 147 |         setLocalTasks((current) => current.map((task) => task.id === taskId ? updater(task) : task));
 148 |         setSelectedTask((current) => current && current.id === taskId ? updater(current) : current);
 149 |     }, []);
 150 | 
 151 |     const updateStatus = useCallback((task: TaskRow, status: string) => {
 152 |         const previous = task;
 153 |         const nextStatus = status as TaskStatus;
 154 | 
 155 |         replaceTask(task.id, (current) => ({
 156 |             ...current,
 157 |             status: nextStatus,
 158 |             completedAt: nextStatus === 'completed' ? new Date().toISOString() : current.completedAt,
 159 |             progress: nextStatus === 'completed' ? 100 : current.progress,
 160 |         }));
 161 | 
 162 |         router.put(`/tasks/${task.id}/status`, { status }, {
 163 |             preserveScroll: true,
 164 |             preserveState: true,
 165 |             onSuccess: () => toast.success('Status updated.'),
 166 |             onError: () => {
 167 |                 replaceTask(task.id, () => previous);
 168 |                 toast.error('Status update failed.');
 169 |             },
 170 |         });
 171 |     }, [replaceTask]);
 172 | 
 173 |     const toggleChecklistItem = useCallback((taskId: number, itemId: number) => {
 174 |         const previous = localTasks.find((task) => task.id === taskId) ?? null;
 175 | 
 176 |         replaceTask(taskId, (task) => {
 177 |             const checklistItems = Array.isArray(task.checklistItems) ? task.checklistItems : [];
 178 |             const nextItems = checklistItems.map((item) => item.id === itemId ? { ...item, isDone: !item.isDone } : item);
 179 |             const progress = nextItems.length > 0
 180 |                 ? Math.round((nextItems.filter((item) => item.isDone).length / nextItems.length) * 100)
 181 |                 : 0;
 182 | 
 183 |             return {
 184 |                 ...task,
 185 |                 checklistItems: nextItems,
 186 |                 progress,
 187 |             };
 188 |         });
 189 | 
 190 |         router.put(`/tasks/${taskId}/checklist/${itemId}/toggle`, {}, {
 191 |             preserveScroll: true,
 192 |             preserveState: true,
 193 |             onError: () => {
 194 |                 if (previous) {
 195 |                     replaceTask(taskId, () => previous);
 196 |                 }
 197 | 
 198 |                 toast.error('Checklist update failed.');
 199 |             },
 200 |         });
 201 |     }, [localTasks, replaceTask]);
 202 | 
 203 |     const addChecklistItem = useCallback((taskId: number, label: string) => {
 204 |         router.post(`/tasks/${taskId}/checklist`, { label }, {
 205 |             preserveScroll: true,
 206 |             onSuccess: () => toast.success('Checklist item added.'),
 207 |             onError: () => toast.error('Checklist item could not be added.'),
 208 |         });
 209 |     }, []);
 210 | 
 211 |     const addComment = useCallback((taskId: number, body: string) => {
 212 |         router.post(`/tasks/${taskId}/comments`, { body, is_note: false }, {
 213 |             preserveScroll: true,
 214 |             onSuccess: () => toast.success('Comment added.'),
 215 |             onError: () => toast.error('Comment could not be added.'),
 216 |         });
 217 |     }, []);
 218 | 
 219 |     const uploadAttachment = useCallback((taskId: number, file: File) => {
 220 |         router.post(`/tasks/${taskId}/attachments`, { file }, {
 221 |             forceFormData: true,
 222 |             preserveScroll: true,
 223 |             onSuccess: () => toast.success('Attachment uploaded.'),
 224 |             onError: () => toast.error('Attachment upload failed.'),
 225 |         });
 226 |     }, []);
 227 | 
 228 |     return (
 229 |         <>
 230 |             <Head title="Tasks" />
 231 |             <AppShell eyebrowKey="nav.tasks" titleKey="nav.tasks" subtitleKey="Tasks and workflow management"
 232 |                 action={
 233 |                     <div className="flex flex-wrap gap-2">
 234 |                         <AppButton variant="secondary" onPress={() => router.visit('/task-requests')}>
 235 |                             Requests
 236 |                         </AppButton>
 237 |                         <AppButton variant="secondary" onPress={() => setRequestOpen(true)}>
 238 |                             <Plus size={16} /> New request
 239 |                         </AppButton>
 240 |                         <AppButton variant="secondary" onPress={() => router.visit('/workload')}>
 241 |                             Workload
 242 |                         </AppButton>
 243 |                         <AppButton variant="primary" onPress={() => { setFormErrors({}); setCreateOpen(true); }}>
 244 |                             <Plus size={16} /> Create task
 245 |                         </AppButton>
 246 |                     </div>
 247 |                 }
 248 |             >
 249 |                 <div className="crm-page">
 250 |                     <section className="grid gap-3 md:grid-cols-2 xl:grid-cols-5">
 251 |                         <OperationsMetric label="Open" value={metrics.open.length} helper="Active operational work" tone="blue" icon={Clock3} />
 252 |                         <OperationsMetric label="Urgent" value={metrics.urgent.length} helper="Urgent or critical impact" tone={metrics.urgent.length ? 'red' : 'green'} icon={Flame} />
 253 |                         <OperationsMetric label="Blocked" value={metrics.blocked.length} helper="Needs management attention" tone={metrics.blocked.length ? 'red' : 'green'} icon={ShieldAlert} />
 254 |                         <OperationsMetric label="Overdue" value={metrics.overdue.length} helper="Past due date" tone={metrics.overdue.length ? 'red' : 'green'} icon={AlertTriangle} />
 255 |                         <OperationsMetric label="Review" value={metrics.review.length} helper={`${metrics.completed.length} completed total`} tone="gold" icon={CheckCircle2} />
 256 |                     </section>
 257 | 
 258 |                     <TaskFilters
 259 |                         filter={filter}
 260 |                         category={category}
 261 |                         query={query}
 262 |                         viewMode={viewMode}
 263 |                         onFilterChange={(f) => { setFilter(f); router.visit(`/tasks?filter=${f}&category=${category}`, { preserveState: true }); }}
 264 |                         onCategoryChange={(c) => { setCategory(c); router.visit(`/tasks?filter=${filter}&category=${c}`, { preserveState: true }); }}
 265 |                         onQueryChange={setQuery}
 266 |                         onViewModeChange={setViewMode}
 267 |                     />
 268 | 
 269 |                     {viewMode === 'board' ? (
 270 |                         <TaskBoard columns={columns} onTaskClick={setSelectedTask} />
 271 |                     ) : viewMode === 'calendar' ? (
 272 |                         <TaskCalendar tasks={filtered} onTaskClick={setSelectedTask} />
 273 |                     ) : (
 274 |                         <TaskList tasks={filtered} columns={columns} onTaskClick={setSelectedTask} onStatusChange={updateStatus} />
 275 |                     )}
 276 | 
 277 |                     <TaskCreateDrawer
 278 |                         isOpen={createOpen}
 279 |                         users={users}
 280 |                         form={form}
 281 |                         formErrors={formErrors}
 282 |                         onOpenChange={(o) => { setCreateOpen(o); if (!o) setFormErrors({}); }}
 283 |                         onFormChange={setForm}
 284 |                         onSubmit={handleCreate}
 285 |                     />
 286 | 
 287 |                     <TaskDetailDrawer
 288 |                         task={selectedTask}
 289 |                         onClose={() => setSelectedTask(null)}
 290 |                         onComplete={(t) => updateStatus(t, 'completed')}
 291 |                         onChecklistToggle={toggleChecklistItem}
 292 |                         onChecklistAdd={addChecklistItem}
 293 |                         onCommentAdd={addComment}
 294 |                         onAttachmentUpload={uploadAttachment}
 295 |                     />
 296 | 
 297 |                     <TaskRequestCreateDrawer
 298 |                         isOpen={requestOpen}
 299 |                         requestTypes={taskRequestTypes}
 300 |                         requestTypeLabels={taskRequestTypeLabels}
 301 |                         options={taskRequestOptions}
 302 |                         onOpenChange={setRequestOpen}
 303 |                     />
 304 |                 </div>
 305 |             </AppShell>
 306 |         </>
 307 |     );
 308 | }

===== resources/js/pages/TaskRequests/Index.tsx =====

   1 | import { Head, router } from '@inertiajs/react';
   2 | import { CheckCircle2, FilePlus2, ListFilter, Plus, XCircle } from 'lucide-react';
   3 | import { useMemo, useState } from 'react';
   4 | import { toast } from 'sonner';
   5 | import { AppShell } from '@/components/layout/AppShell';
   6 | import { TaskRequestCreateDrawer, type TaskRequestOptions } from '@/features/tasks/components/TaskRequestCreateDrawer';
   7 | 
   8 | type TaskRequestRow = {
   9 |     id: number;
  10 |     requestNumber: string;
  11 |     requestType: string;
  12 |     title: string;
  13 |     description: string | null;
  14 |     status: string;
  15 |     convertedTaskId: number | null;
  16 |     client: { id: number; name: string } | null;
  17 |     dossier: { id: number; number: string; object: string } | null;
  18 |     updatedAt: string;
  19 | };
  20 | 
  21 | type PageProps = {
  22 |     taskRequests: TaskRequestRow[];
  23 |     requestTypes: string[];
  24 |     requestStatuses: string[];
  25 |     requestTypeLabels: Record<string, string>;
  26 |     requestStatusLabels: Record<string, string>;
  27 |     taskRequestOptions: TaskRequestOptions;
  28 | };
  29 | 
  30 | const STATUS_COLORS: Record<string, string> = {
  31 |     submitted: 'border-blue-400/20 bg-blue-400/10 text-blue-300',
  32 |     accepted: 'border-emerald-400/20 bg-emerald-400/10 text-emerald-300',
  33 |     rejected: 'border-red-400/20 bg-red-400/10 text-red-300',
  34 |     converted: 'border-amber-400/20 bg-amber-400/10 text-amber-300',
  35 | };
  36 | 
  37 | function postAction(url: string, success: string, data: Record<string, string> = {}) {
  38 |     router.post(url, data, {
  39 |         preserveScroll: true,
  40 |         onSuccess: () => toast.success(success),
  41 |         onError: () => toast.error('Action failed.'),
  42 |     });
  43 | }
  44 | 
  45 | export default function TaskRequestsIndex({ taskRequests, requestTypes, requestStatuses, requestTypeLabels, requestStatusLabels, taskRequestOptions }: PageProps) {
  46 |     const [activeStatus, setActiveStatus] = useState('all');
  47 |     const [createOpen, setCreateOpen] = useState(false);
  48 |     const filteredRequests = useMemo(() => {
  49 |         return activeStatus === 'all' ? taskRequests : taskRequests.filter((item) => item.status === activeStatus);
  50 |     }, [activeStatus, taskRequests]);
  51 | 
  52 |     const counts = useMemo(() => {
  53 |         return taskRequests.reduce<Record<string, number>>((carry, item) => {
  54 |             carry[item.status] = (carry[item.status] ?? 0) + 1;
  55 |             return carry;
  56 |         }, {});
  57 |     }, [taskRequests]);
  58 | 
  59 |     const reject = (item: TaskRequestRow) => {
  60 |         const reason = window.prompt('Reason for rejection');
  61 |         if (reason === null) return;
  62 | 
  63 |         postAction(`/task-requests/${item.id}/reject`, 'Request rejected.', { reason });
  64 |     };
  65 | 
  66 |     return (
  67 |         <>
  68 |             <Head title="Task requests" />
  69 |             <AppShell eyebrowKey="nav.tasks" titleKey="nav.tasks" subtitleKey="Operations intake requests">
  70 |                 <div className="crm-page">
  71 |                     <section className="crm-panel overflow-hidden">
  72 |                         <div className="flex flex-wrap items-center justify-between gap-3 border-b border-[var(--crm-border)] p-4">
  73 |                             <div>
  74 |                                 <p className="crm-eyebrow">Operations intake</p>
  75 |                                 <h1 className="mt-1 text-xl font-black text-[var(--crm-text)]">Task requests</h1>
  76 |                                 <p className="mt-1 text-xs text-[var(--crm-muted)]">Review office requests and convert accepted work into real tasks.</p>
  77 |                             </div>
  78 |                             <div className="flex flex-wrap gap-2">
  79 |                                 <button type="button" className="crm-action-button" onClick={() => router.visit('/tasks')}>
  80 |                                     Open tasks
  81 |                                 </button>
  82 |                                 <button type="button" className="crm-action-button border-[var(--crm-gold)] bg-[var(--crm-gold)] text-black hover:bg-[var(--crm-gold)]" onClick={() => setCreateOpen(true)}>
  83 |                                     <Plus size={14} />
  84 |                                     New request
  85 |                                 </button>
  86 |                             </div>
  87 |                         </div>
  88 | 
  89 |                         <div className="flex flex-wrap items-center gap-2 border-b border-[var(--crm-border)] p-3">
  90 |                             <span className="mr-1 inline-flex items-center gap-1.5 text-xs font-black uppercase tracking-[0.14em] text-[var(--crm-muted)]">
  91 |                                 <ListFilter size={13} /> Status
  92 |                             </span>
  93 |                             {['all', ...requestStatuses].map((status) => (
  94 |                                 <button key={status} type="button" onClick={() => setActiveStatus(status)}
  95 |                                     className={`rounded-full border px-3 py-1.5 text-xs font-black transition ${
  96 |                                         activeStatus === status
  97 |                                             ? 'border-[var(--crm-gold)] bg-[var(--crm-gold)] text-black'
  98 |                                             : 'border-[var(--crm-border)] bg-[var(--crm-surface)] text-[var(--crm-text-muted)] hover:border-[var(--crm-gold)] hover:text-[var(--crm-gold)]'
  99 |                                     }`}>
 100 |                                     {status === 'all' ? 'All' : requestStatusLabels[status] ?? status}
 101 |                                     <span className="ml-1 opacity-70">{status === 'all' ? taskRequests.length : counts[status] ?? 0}</span>
 102 |                                 </button>
 103 |                             ))}
 104 |                         </div>
 105 | 
 106 |                         <div className="grid gap-2 p-4">
 107 |                             {filteredRequests.map((item) => {
 108 |                                 const isClosed = item.status === 'rejected' || item.status === 'converted';
 109 |                                 const statusClass = STATUS_COLORS[item.status] ?? 'border-zinc-400/20 bg-zinc-400/10 text-zinc-300';
 110 | 
 111 |                                 return (
 112 |                                 <div key={item.id} className="grid gap-3 rounded-xl border border-[var(--crm-border)] bg-[var(--crm-elevated)] p-3 lg:grid-cols-[1fr_auto] lg:items-center">
 113 |                                     <div className="min-w-0">
 114 |                                         <div className="flex flex-wrap items-center gap-2">
 115 |                                             <p className="truncate text-sm font-black">{item.title}</p>
 116 |                                             <span className={`rounded-full border px-2 py-0.5 text-[10px] font-black uppercase ${statusClass}`}>
 117 |                                                 {requestStatusLabels[item.status] ?? item.status}
 118 |                                             </span>
 119 |                                         </div>
 120 |                                         <p className="mt-1 text-xs text-[var(--crm-muted)]">
 121 |                                             {item.requestNumber} / {requestTypeLabels[item.requestType] ?? item.requestType} / Updated {item.updatedAt}
 122 |                                         </p>
 123 |                                         {item.client || item.dossier ? (
 124 |                                             <p className="mt-1 text-xs text-[var(--crm-text-muted)]">
 125 |                                                 {[item.client?.name, item.dossier?.number].filter(Boolean).join(' / ')}
 126 |                                             </p>
 127 |                                         ) : null}
 128 |                                         {item.description ? <p className="mt-2 line-clamp-2 text-xs text-[var(--crm-text-muted)]">{item.description}</p> : null}
 129 |                                     </div>
 130 |                                     <div className="flex flex-wrap gap-2 lg:justify-end">
 131 |                                         {item.convertedTaskId ? (
 132 |                                             <button type="button" className="crm-action-button" onClick={() => router.visit('/tasks')}>
 133 |                                                 Open tasks
 134 |                                             </button>
 135 |                                         ) : null}
 136 |                                         <button type="button" disabled={isClosed || item.status === 'accepted'} className="crm-action-button disabled:cursor-not-allowed disabled:opacity-45"
 137 |                                             onClick={() => postAction(`/task-requests/${item.id}/accept`, 'Request accepted.')}>
 138 |                                             <CheckCircle2 size={14} />
 139 |                                             Accept
 140 |                                         </button>
 141 |                                         <button type="button" disabled={isClosed} className="crm-action-button disabled:cursor-not-allowed disabled:opacity-45"
 142 |                                             onClick={() => postAction(`/task-requests/${item.id}/convert`, 'Request converted to task.')}>
 143 |                                             <FilePlus2 size={14} />
 144 |                                             Convert
 145 |                                         </button>
 146 |                                         <button type="button" disabled={isClosed} className="crm-action-button border-red-400/20 bg-red-400/10 text-red-300 disabled:cursor-not-allowed disabled:opacity-45"
 147 |                                             onClick={() => reject(item)}>
 148 |                                             <XCircle size={14} />
 149 |                                             Reject
 150 |                                         </button>
 151 |                                     </div>
 152 |                                 </div>
 153 |                             )})}
 154 |                             {filteredRequests.length === 0 ? (
 155 |                                 <div className="rounded-xl border border-dashed border-[var(--crm-border)] p-8 text-center text-sm text-[var(--crm-muted)]">
 156 |                                     No task requests in this status.
 157 |                                 </div>
 158 |                             ) : null}
 159 |                         </div>
 160 |                     </section>
 161 | 
 162 |                     <TaskRequestCreateDrawer
 163 |                         isOpen={createOpen}
 164 |                         requestTypes={requestTypes}
 165 |                         requestTypeLabels={requestTypeLabels}
 166 |                         options={taskRequestOptions}
 167 |                         onOpenChange={setCreateOpen}
 168 |                     />
 169 |                 </div>
 170 |             </AppShell>
 171 |         </>
 172 |     );
 173 | }

===== resources/js/pages/Workload/Index.tsx =====

   1 | import { Head } from '@inertiajs/react';
   2 | import { AppShell } from '@/components/layout/AppShell';
   3 | 
   4 | type WorkloadRow = {
   5 |     userId: number;
   6 |     name: string;
   7 |     email: string;
   8 |     openTasks: number;
   9 |     urgentTasks: number;
  10 |     blockedTasks: number;
  11 |     overdueTasks: number;
  12 | };
  13 | 
  14 | export default function WorkloadIndex({ workload }: { workload: WorkloadRow[] }) {
  15 |     return (
  16 |         <>
  17 |             <Head title="Workload" />
  18 |             <AppShell eyebrowKey="nav.tasks" titleKey="nav.tasks" subtitleKey="Team workload summary">
  19 |                 <div className="crm-page">
  20 |                     <section className="crm-panel overflow-hidden">
  21 |                         <div className="border-b border-[var(--crm-border)] p-4">
  22 |                             <p className="crm-eyebrow">Operations</p>
  23 |                             <h1 className="mt-1 text-xl font-black text-[var(--crm-text)]">Workload</h1>
  24 |                         </div>
  25 |                         <div className="overflow-x-auto">
  26 |                             <table className="crm-table">
  27 |                                 <thead>
  28 |                                     <tr>
  29 |                                         <th>User</th>
  30 |                                         <th>Open</th>
  31 |                                         <th>Urgent</th>
  32 |                                         <th>Blocked</th>
  33 |                                         <th>Overdue</th>
  34 |                                     </tr>
  35 |                                 </thead>
  36 |                                 <tbody>
  37 |                                     {workload.map((row) => (
  38 |                                         <tr key={row.userId}>
  39 |                                             <td>
  40 |                                                 <div className="font-bold">{row.name}</div>
  41 |                                                 <div className="text-xs text-[var(--crm-muted)]">{row.email}</div>
  42 |                                             </td>
  43 |                                             <td>{row.openTasks}</td>
  44 |                                             <td>{row.urgentTasks}</td>
  45 |                                             <td>{row.blockedTasks}</td>
  46 |                                             <td>{row.overdueTasks}</td>
  47 |                                         </tr>
  48 |                                     ))}
  49 |                                 </tbody>
  50 |                             </table>
  51 |                         </div>
  52 |                     </section>
  53 |                 </div>
  54 |             </AppShell>
  55 |         </>
  56 |     );
  57 | }
